The issue occurs because `reshardLoop()` calls `t.shards.stop()` which attempts to acquire an exclusive write lock (`s.mtx.Lock()`), while concurrently, enqueue operations hold read locks (`s.mtx.RLock()`) and may block indefinitely on full queues. This lock-order deadlock prevents resharded shards from ever flushing their data, leaving `prometheus_remote_storage_samples_pending` stuck at values > 0.\n\n## Root Cause\n\n**File:** `storage/remote/queue_manager.go`\n**Key Functions:** `reshardLoop()` (line 1184), `shards.stop()` (line 1269), `shards.enqueue()` (line 1312)\n\n### Mechanism\n\nThe deadlock occurs in the following sequence:\n\n1. **`reshardLoop()` initiates resharding** (line 1189-1194):\n   ```go\n   case numShards := <-t.reshardChan:\n       t.shards.stop()           // Line 1193: Calls stop()\n       t.shards.start(numShards)\n   ```\n\n2. **`shards.stop()` attempts exclusive lock** (line 1269-1282):\n   ```go\n   s.mtx.RLock()              // Line 1274: First, read lock to close softShutdown\n   close(s.softShutdown)\n   s.mtx.RUnlock()\n\n   s.mtx.Lock()               // Line 1281: CRITICAL - Try to acquire write lock\n   defer s.mtx.Unlock()\n   for _, queue := range s.queues {\n       go queue.FlushAndShutdown(s.done)\n   }\n   ```\n\n3. **Concurrent enqueue operations hold read locks** (line 1312-1340):\n   ```go\n   func (s *shards) enqueue(ref chunks.HeadSeriesRef, data timeSeries) bool {\n       s.mtx.RLock()          // Line 1313: Acquire read lock\n       defer s.mtx.RUnlock()\n       shard := uint64(ref) % uint64(len(s.queues))\n       select {\n       case <-s.softShutdown:\n           return false\n       default:\n           appended := s.queues[shard].Append(data)  // Line 1320: Can BLOCK if queue is full\n           // ...\n           s.qm.metrics.highestTimestamp.Set(...)\n           s.qm.dataIn.incr(1)\n       }\n   }\n   ```\n\n### Why It's Intermittent\n\nThe issue is **timing-dependent**:\n\n- **Happy path:** If enqueue operations release their read locks before `shards.stop()` tries to acquire the write lock, resharding succeeds.\n- **Deadlock path:** If an enqueue operation:\n  1. Acquires `s.mtx.RLock()` at line 1313\n  2. Calls `s.queues[shard].Append(data)` at line 1320, which **blocks** because the queue is full\n  3. The original pre-change code tracked these metrics at the `WriteStorage` level (outside the shard lock), avoiding the lock-holding scenario.\n\n## Evidence\n\n### Code References\n\n1. **Deadlock Path:**\n   - `reshardLoop()`: line 1184-1198\n   - `shards.stop()` write lock attempt: line 1281\n   - `shards.enqueue()` read lock + potential block: lines 1313-1340\n\n2. **The Critical Lock Sequence:**\n   - `enqueue()` acquires RLock at line 1313\n   - `enqueue()` blocks on `queue.Append()` at line 1320 (while holding RLock)\n   - `shards.stop()` waits for write lock at line 1281 (blocked by RLock holders)\n\n3. **Metric Updates Inside Lock:**\n   - Lines 1337-1338 update metrics while holding the read lock\n   - These operations complete only after `queue.Append()` succeeds\n   - But `Append()` can block if queue is at capacity\n\n### Commit History\n\n- **Introduced:** `184c7eb91` (Sept 2025) - Moved `dataIn.incr()` and `highestTimestamp.Set()` into `enqueue()` path\n- **Fixed:** `6806b68f9` (Oct 2025, #17412) - Reverted the problematic changes\n- **Test Issues:** Multiple test skips/re-enables (commits `35c3232a2`, `26f8c92de`, `e7999528f`) indicate the issue remained flaky even after revert\n\n## Affected Components\n\n1. **`storage/remote/queue_manager.go`**\n   - `QueueManager.updateShardsLoop()` - Triggers resharding\n   - `QueueManager.reshardLoop()` - Executes resharding\n   - `shards.enqueue()` - Holds read locks during queue operations\n   - `shards.stop()` - Attempts write lock during shutdown\n   - `shards.start()` - Initiates new shards with new queues\n\n2. **Metrics Affected**\n   - `prometheus_remote_storage_samples_pending` - Stuck at > 0\n   - `prometheus_remote_storage_queue_highest_timestamp_seconds` - May not update if shards are stalled\n\n## Recommendation\n\n### Primary Fix Strategy\n\nThe core issue is **holding a lock while performing potentially-blocking I/O**. The proper fix is to separate metrics tracking from the enqueue lock critical section:\n\n1. **Move metrics updates outside the lock:** Decouple `dataIn.incr()` and `highestTimestamp.Set()` from the `enqueue()` method so they don't block other operations that need the lock.\n\n2. **Alternative approaches:**\n   - Use a separate non-blocking metrics update mechanism (atomic operations or lock-free queue)\n   - Change the lock strategy to non-blocking or use a different synchronization primitive\n   - Ensure enqueue operations never block on queue capacity (use non-blocking send or separate queue logic)\n\n3. **The reverted code (commit 6806b68f9) shows the correct approach:**\n   - `dataIn` tracking moved back to shard level (outside of `enqueue()` critical path)\n   - `highestTimestamp` updates moved to queue manager metrics (no lock holding)\n   - Prevents read lock holders from blocking on queue operations\n\n### Diagnostic Steps\n\nTo confirm the root cause in production:\n\n1. **Enable debug logging** to see \"Currently resharding, skipping\" messages (line 1117)\n2. **Monitor goroutine profiles** to detect blocked goroutines in `shards.stop()` and `shards.enqueue()`\n3. **Check pprof mutex contention** to identify lock conflicts\n4. **Observe these metrics:**\n   - `prometheus_remote_storage_samples_pending` - Should return to 0 after resharding completes\n   - `prometheus_remote_storage_queue_num_shards` - Should change to reflect new shard count\n   - `prometheus_remote_storage_desired_num_shards` - Should guide resharding decisions\n\n5. **Look for deadlock pattern in logs:**\n   - \"Remote storage resharding from N to M\"\n   - Followed by stuck metrics (samples_pending stays constant)\n   - No further resharding events or shard updates\n\n### Code Locations to Review\n\n1. While blocked with the read lock held, the write lock in `shards.stop()` (line 1281) **waits indefinitely** for all read locks to be released\n  4. New enqueue attempts are also **blocked waiting for the write lock** to release\n  5. The queue never gets flushed because the shards are stuck in shutdown\n  6. Result: `prometheus_remote_storage_samples_pending` remains > 0 indefinitely\n\n### Historical Context\n\nThis issue was introduced in commit `184c7eb91` (\"storage/remote: compute highestTimestamp and dataIn at QueueManager level\") and reverted in commit `6806b68f9` (#17412).\n\nThe problematic change moved `dataIn` tracking and `highestTimestamp` updates into the `enqueue()` function path (line 1337-1338). The original pre-change code tracked these metrics at the `WriteStorage` level (outside the shard lock), avoiding the lock-holding scenario.\n\n## Evidence\n\n### Code References\n\n1. **Deadlock Path:**\n   - `reshardLoop()`: line 1184-1198\n   - `shards.stop()` write lock attempt: line 1281\n   - `shards.enqueue()` read lock + potential block: lines 1313-1340\n\n2. **The Critical Lock Sequence:**\n   - `enqueue()` acquires RLock at line 1313\n   - `enqueue()` blocks on `queue.Append()` at line 1320 (while holding RLock)\n   - `shards.stop()` waits for write lock at line 1281 (blocked by RLock holders)\n\n3. **Metric Updates Inside Lock:**\n   - Lines 1337-1338 update metrics while holding the read lock\n   - These operations complete only after `queue.Append()` succeeds\n   - But `Append()` can block if queue is at capacity\n\n### Commit History\n\n- **Introduced:** `184c7eb91` (Sept 2025) - Moved `dataIn.incr()` and `highestTimestamp.Set()` into `enqueue()` path\n- **Fixed:** `6806b68f9` (Oct 2025, #17412) - Reverted the problematic changes\n- **Test Issues:** Multiple test skips/re-enables (commits `35c3232a2`, `26f8c92de`, `e7999528f`) indicate the issue remained flaky even after revert\n\n## Affected Components\n\n1. **`storage/remote/queue_manager.go`**\n   - `QueueManager.updateShardsLoop()` - Triggers resharding\n   - `QueueManager.reshardLoop()` - Executes resharding\n   - `shards.enqueue()` - Holds read locks during queue operations\n   - `shards.stop()` - Attempts write lock during shutdown\n   - `shards.start()` - Initiates new shards with new queues\n\n2. **Metrics Affected**\n   - `prometheus_remote_storage_samples_pending` - Stuck at > 0\n   - `prometheus_remote_storage_queue_highest_timestamp_seconds` - May not update if shards are stalled\n\n## Recommendation\n\n### Primary Fix Strategy\n\nThe core issue is **holding a lock while performing potentially-blocking I/O**. The proper fix is to separate metrics tracking from the enqueue lock critical section:\n\n1. **Move metrics updates outside the lock:** Decouple `dataIn.incr()` and `highestTimestamp.Set()` from the `enqueue()` method so they don't block other operations that need the lock.\n\n2. **Alternative approaches:**\n   - Use a separate non-blocking metrics update mechanism (atomic operations or lock-free queue)\n   - Change the lock strategy to non-blocking or use a different synchronization primitive\n   - Ensure enqueue operations never block on queue capacity (use non-blocking send or separate queue logic)\n\n3. **The reverted code (commit 6806b68f9) shows the correct approach:**\n   - `dataIn` tracking moved back to shard level (outside of `enqueue()` critical path)\n   - `highestTimestamp` updates moved to queue manager metrics (no lock holding)\n   - Prevents read lock holders from blocking on queue operations\n\n### Diagnostic Steps\n\nTo confirm the root cause in production:\n\n1. **Enable debug logging** to see \"Currently resharding, skipping\" messages (line 1117)\n2. **Monitor goroutine profiles** to detect blocked goroutines in `shards.stop()` and `shards.enqueue()`\n3. **Check pprof mutex contention** to identify lock conflicts\n4. **Observe these metrics:**\n   - `prometheus_remote_storage_samples_pending` - Should return to 0 after resharding completes\n   - `prometheus_remote_storage_queue_num_shards` - Should change to reflect new shard count\n   - `prometheus_remote_storage_desired_num_shards` - Should guide resharding decisions\n\n5. **Look for deadlock pattern in logs:**\n   - \"Remote storage resharding from N to M\"\n   - Followed by stuck metrics (samples_pending stays constant)\n   - No further resharding events or shard updates\n\n### Code Locations to Review\n\n1. Queue manager initialization: `NewQueueManager()` line 471\n2. Shard start/stop lifecycle: `shards.start()` line 1243, `shards.stop()` line 1269\n3. Enqueue critical section: `shards.enqueue()` line 1312-1340\n4. Metric updates: Lines 1337-1338 (highestTimestamp, dataIn tracking)\n5. Lock strategy: `shards` struct locks (`s.mtx`) at lines 1209-1210\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
